# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Estimating the asymptotics of integer partitions in intermediate dimensions ($d = 3,4,5,6$)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03034)
*Avinandan Mondal*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文通过自适应权重学习加马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）模拟（N=15000），数值估计了维度d=3,4,5,6的整数分拆的渐近行为，并数值上证明了中间维度下整数分拆的渐近增长速度严格快于MacMahon数，给出了各维度的渐近系数及置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前结果已证明维度d≥7的整数分拆渐近增长严格快于MacMahon数，而d=1,2时两者一致。d=3,4,5,6作为中间维度，二者渐近性的比较问题仍是开放问题。本文旨在通过数值方法回答这一开放问题。

Method: 采用自适应权重学习后再进行常规MCMC步骤的方法，对d=3,4,5,6共四个维度的整数分拆进行模拟至N=15000，并假设极限存在的前提下，数值估计\log p_d(n)的渐近系数及部分次主导渐近项。

Result: 数值结果表明中间维度的整数分拆渐近增长均快于MacMahon数。具体估计：d=3时极限系数为1.8196±0.0019，d=4为1.7215±0.0045，d=5为1.6521±0.0059，d=6为1.652±0.021。这些数值全部大于对应的MacMahon主导阶渐近系数（1.7898, 1.6614, 1.5737, 1.509）。此外还得到了各维度部分次主导渐近项的估计。

Conclusion: 研究数值上证实了在中间维度d=3,4,5,6下，整数分拆的渐近增长速度严格快于MacMahon数，填补了此前仅对d=1,2和d≥7已知而未解的中间维度空白，并为相关的严格数学证明提供了数值证据和猜想方向。

Abstract: It was recently shown by Yeliussizov \cite{Yeliussizov} that integer partitions in dimensions $d \geq 7$ asymptotically grow strictly faster than MacMahon numbers. As MacMahon numbers match with integer partitions in dimensions $d = 1,2$, the comparison of asymptotics of integer partitions with MacMahon numbers in intermediate dimensions ($d = 3,4,5,6$) is an open question. In this work, we perform Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) simulations till $N=15000$ by using adaptive weight learning followed by conventional MCMC steps to numerically estimate the asymptotics of integer partitions in these intermediate dimensions. We numerically establish that in these intermediate dimensions, partitions asymptotically grow faster than MacMahon numbers. More specifically, assuming that the limits exist, we show: $\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-3/4}\log p_3(n) = 1.8196 \pm 0.0019$, $\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-4/5}\log p_4(n) = 1.7215 \pm 0.0045$, $\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-5/6}\log p_5(n) = 1.6521 \pm 0.0059$, and $\lim_{n\to\infty}\log n^{-6/7}p_6(n) = 1.652 \pm 0.021$ for partitions in dimensions $d=3,4,5,$ and $6$ respectively. These numbers are all larger than MacMahon leading order asymptotic coefficients of $1.7898, 1.6614, 1.5737,$ and $1.509$ respectively. Additionally, we also find estimates for some of the sub-leading asymptotic terms in $\log p_d(n)$ in each of the dimensions.

</details>


### [158] [Computational Algorithms for Invariant Reduction of Variational Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03197)
*Kostya Druzhkov,Alexey Shevyakov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究偏微分方程（PDE）的对称约化如何保留几何结构（守恒律、变分结构、Hamilton型结构）。通过将变分p-形式解释为增广系统（度数平移的切系统）中的守恒律，其中扰动变量被视为反交换（格拉斯曼）变量，作者将守恒律、变分1-形式和预辛结构的约化统一为单一算法流程，并给出了三种算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDE的对称约化比其他类型的约化保留了更多几何结构（不变量守恒律、变分结构以及在某些条件下的Hamilton型结构），但这些结构的显式约化计算缺乏系统化的算法实现。作者旨在建立统一的计算框架，使这些几何结构的约化可以通过统一的算法过程自动完成。

Method: 将变分p-形式视为（度数平移的）切系统——由原始方程及其线性化组成的增广系统——中的守恒律，其中扰动变量作为反交换变量处理。这样，约化问题被重新表述为分类合（graded-commutative）框架下守恒律理论中的适用概念。据此提出三种算法：（i）基于同伦的约化算法，适用于演化方程组，已在Maple中实现；（ii）适用于一般ℓ-正规系统（p>0）的下降约化算法；（iii）在适当条件下适用于点对称的简化约化算法，可描述为涉及更少独立变量的系统约化。

Result: 作者给出了三种计算算法，并在多个示例上进行了验证：一维和二维空间中的非线性演化方程、Laplace方程、不可压缩Euler方程以及Pavlov方程的余切系统。

Conclusion: 通过在增广（切）系统中将变分p-形式重新解释为守恒律，作者成功地将守恒律、变分1-形式和预辛结构的对称约化统一为单一算法流程，并提供了适用范围不同的三种算法及相应的计算实现。

Abstract: Symmetry reductions of partial differential equations (PDEs) inherit more geometric structures than other types of reductions: invariant conservation laws, variational structures, and, under suitable conditions, Hamiltonian-type structures of the original model descend to the reduced model through the mechanism of invariant reduction. This paper develops computational algorithms that carry out such reductions explicitly. We use the interpretation of variational $p$-forms as conservation laws of an enlarged system --- the (degree-shifted) tangent system --- consisting of the original equations together with their linearizations, in which the perturbation variables are treated as anticommuting. This allows us to formulate the algorithms using well-known concepts from the theory of conservation laws, naturally adapted to the graded-commutative setting. The reduction of conservation laws, variational $1$-forms, and presymplectic structures thereby becomes a single algorithmic procedure. We present (i) a homotopy-based reduction algorithm for systems of evolution equations, implemented in Maple; (ii) a descent reduction algorithm applicable to general $\ell$-normal systems for $p>0$; and (iii) a simple reduction algorithm available for point symmetries under suitable conditions, including the description of reductions in terms of systems involving fewer independent variables. Examples include nonlinear evolution equations in one and two spatial dimensions, the Laplace equation, the incompressible Euler equations, and the cotangent system of Pavlov's equation.

</details>


### [159] [Optimal bounds for embedded eigenvalues of one-dimensional discrete Schrödinger operators with decaying potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03224)
*Jifeng Chu,Wencai Liu,Kang Lyu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一维离散薛定谔算子H=Δ+V，其中V(n)=O(1)/(1+n)，确定了渐近系数控制嵌入特征值存在与不存在的尖锐相变条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在连续情形下，Schrödinger算子的嵌入特征值问题已有较为成熟的理论，但在离散情形下，当势能具有长程衰减（如O(1)/(1+n)）时，嵌入特征值是否存在取决于势能的渐近系数。本文旨在刻画这一临界阈值，填补离散算子嵌入特征值理论中关于临界衰减阶的空白。

Method: 通过分析一维离散Schrödinger算子在ℓ²(N)上带有自伴边界条件的谱性质，对势能V(n)进行O(1)/(1+n)阶的渐近展开，利用谱理论、散射理论及渐近分析方法，考察其渐近系数的临界值对嵌入特征值存在性的影响。

Result: 确定了嵌入特征值存在与不存在的尖锐相变点，即势能V(n)的渐近系数存在一个精确的阈值，使得系数超过该阈值时嵌入特征值存在，低于该阈值时不存在。

Conclusion: 本文给出了离散Schrödinger算子在临界衰减势下嵌入特征值存在性的完整刻画，确定了势能渐近系数的精确临界值，为后续相关研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider one-dimensional discrete Schrödinger operators \begin{align}
  Hu(n)=(Δ+V)u(n)\nonumber \end{align}
  on $\ell^2(\mathbb{N})$ with a self-adjoint boundary condition at $n=0$, where $Δ$ denotes the discrete Laplacian and $V(n)$ is a real-valued perturbation satisfying
  $$V(n)=\frac{O(1)}{1+n}.$$ We determine the sharp transition for the asymptotic coefficient of \(V\) governing the existence and nonexistence of embedded eigenvalues.

</details>


### [160] [Controllability, returning waves and scattering without reverberation in 3D acoustic dynamic system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03536)
*Mikhail I. Belishev,Aleksei F. Vakulenko*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究三维空间中的动态声学散射系统，由波动方程控制，分析了当哈密顿算子具有束缚态时系统可控性的丧失问题，并发现了一种无须余响振动地离开非均匀区域的有限能量波现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究动态声学散射系统在势函数q具有束缚态时的可控性变化，以及由此产生的物理效应——有限能量波在无余响的情况下通过非均匀区域，这在数学上与时间反演镜（波前反转）有相似但更深刻的联系。

Method: 从波动方程出发，考虑紧支撑势q和无穷远源f产生的入射球面波，定义可控性为所有有限能量控制产生的波场覆盖L2(R^3)。通过分析哈密顿量H=-Δ+q的束缚态存在性，证明存在某些焦点位置a使得系统重新聚焦时失去可控性，从而构造出在时间和空间锥体中同时消失的特殊波场。

Result: 证明了当哈密顿量H=-Δ+q具有束缚态时，空间中存在某些点a，使得系统在这些点重新聚焦时丧失可控性。由此推导出存在有限能量的波场u^f，它们同时在过去和未来锥体|x|<±t中消失，并能在不产生余响的情况下离开q的非均匀区域。该效应与波前反转（时间反演镜）类似，但从数学角度来看更具深刻意义。

Conclusion: 系统的可控性与势函数的束缚态结构密切相关：束缚态的存在会破坏系统在某些重聚焦点上的完全可控性，并诱发一种特殊的"无余响穿越"物理效应，这种效应在数学上比时间反演镜更具深刻性。

Abstract: The dynamic acoustic scattering system is governed by the wave equation $u_{tt}-Δu+qu=0$ in $\Bbb R^3,\,\,\,-\infty<t<\infty$, with a compactly supported potential $q$ and infinitely distant sources (controls) $f$, which initiate incoming spherical waves $u=u^f(x,t)$ provided $u^f\big|_{|x|<-t,\,\,\,t<0}=0$. These waves are focused at $x=0$ and fill up the whole space at the moment $t=0$. The system is {\it controllable} if the set of waves $u^f(\cdot,0)$ produced by all finite energy controls $f$, covers the space $L_2(\Bbb R^3)$.
  As we show, if the Hamiltonian $H=-Δ+q$ has the bound states, then in the space the points $a$ appear such that the system, being refocused at $x=a$, loses controllability. The latter leads to a physical effect: the waves $u^f$ of finite energy appear, which vanish simultaneously in the past and future cones $|x|<\pm\, t$ and leave the region of inhomogeneity of $q$ without reverberation. This effect has some similarities with the wavefront reversal (Time Reversing Mirror), but is more meaningful from a mathematical point of view.

</details>


### [161] [The conductivity matrix at the topological phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03832)
*Simone Fabbri,Giovanna Marcelli,Alessandro Giuliani*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对一类二维无相互作用的晶格半金属电子系统，利用欧几里得多体形式主义推导出完整的电导率矩阵显式公式。研究发现纵向电导率仅取决于费米能级处锥形色散的形状，而横向电导率同时由锥形结构和邻近两个拓扑相的性质决定。在一般半金属态中电导率不被量子化，但在费米点处线性化哈密顿量具有涌现旋转对称性时，电导率矩阵的普适性得以恢复。作者用该公式计算了量子霍尔流体在拓扑相变处的电导率矩阵，展示了横向电导率呈现半整数e²/h量子化和依赖于外部应变参数的两种情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半金属态（狄拉克/外尔锥接触点）通常出现在不同整数量子霍尔相之间的相变点，其电导率行为在过渡点附近的普适性尚不清晰。作者希望推导一个通用的显式公式来完整描述二维无相互作用半金属系统的电导率矩阵，从而阐明纵向和横向电导率在半金属态下的量子化与非量子化特性。

Method: 作者采用欧几里得多体形式主义（Euclidean many-body formalism）推导完整的电导率矩阵公式。具体思路是：(1)考虑两个布洛赫带在费米能处以圆锥形交叉的一般半金属晶格模型；(2)系统分析纵向和横向电导率如何依赖费米点周围的锥形结构和邻近拓扑相的拓扑性质；(3)在假设线性化哈密顿量具有涌现旋转对称性的条件下考察电导率矩阵的普适性恢复；(4)将公式应用到多个量子霍尔流体的物理模型中以检验结果。

Result: 得到了完整电导率矩阵的显式公式。纵向电导率仅由费米能级处锥形色散的形状决定；横向电导率则由锥形结构和两个邻近拓扑相共同决定。在半金属态中，纵向和横向电导率一般均不会量子化为整数倍e²/h，但在涌现旋转对称性条件下电导率矩阵恢复普适性。对多个量子霍尔流体模型的计算展示了横向电导率有时呈现半整数e²/h的量子化（如1/2的整数倍），有时则连续依赖于外部应变参数，揭示了其普适性与非普适性的微妙差异。

Conclusion: 本文为二维无相互作用半金属系统的电导率矩阵提供了统一的理论框架，揭示了电导率的普适性依赖于线性化哈密顿量的涌现对称性这一关键条件。该工作澄清了量子霍尔相变点处电导率行为的普适性边界，为理解拓扑相变临界点的输运性质提供了重要理论指导。

Abstract: For a general class of two-dimensional non-interacting semi-metallic electron systems on a lattice, we derive an explicit formula for the whole conductivity matrix, by using Euclidean many-body formalism. The semi-metallic phase takes place whenever two Bloch bands touch at the Fermi energy with conical intersections and generally occurs at the transition between distinct integer quantum Hall phases. Unlike the longitudinal conductivity (which depends solely on the shape of the cones at the Fermi level), the transverse conductivity is remarkably determined both by the conical structures of the bands and by the nature of the two nearby topological phases. Generically, in the semi-metallic state, neither the longitudinal nor the transverse conductivities are quantized in integer multiples of a universal conductivity quantum; however, universality of the conductivity matrix is restored under the assumption of emergent rotational symmetry of the linearized Hamiltonian at the Fermi points. Using our formula, we compute the conductivity matrix for several physically relevant models of quantum Hall fluids at the topological phase transition, exhibiting cases where the transverse conductivity is quantized in half-integer multiples of $e^2/h$ and cases where it depends continuously on an external strain parameter, thus shedding light on its universality or non-universality features.

</details>


### [162] [Classification of abstract Bose field models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03863)
*Yasumichi Matsuzawa*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子场论中，对一类抽象的玻色场模型（包括抽象自由玻色场模型、抽象van Hove-Miyatake模型及红外重整化的van Hove-Miyatake模型）进行了酉等价分类，并将该分类应用于二次相互作用模型的分类，回答了A. Arai关于红外重整化van Hove-Miyatake模型分类的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子场论中玻色场模型虽被广泛研究，但关于其酉等价分类的系统性结果仍不完整，尤其是涉及红外重整化的van Hove-Miyatake模型时，其分类问题一直悬而未决。Arai曾提出关于该类模型分类的开放问题，本研究旨在为该问题提供解答，并建立更广范围内的统一分类框架。

Method: 论文将关注点限定在一类抽象的玻色场模型上，涵盖抽象自由玻色场模型、抽象van Hove-Miyatake模型以及红外重整化van Hove-Miyatake模型，通过对这些模型施加适当的数学结构条件，建立它们之间的酉等价关系判定准则，并将所得分类结果进一步推广到二次相互作用模型的分类中。

Result: 作者成功对上述提及的整个玻色场模型类别给出了酉等价分类，并利用该分类结果实现了对二次相互作用模型的完整分类，最终直接解答了A. Arai关于红外重整化van Hove-Miyatake模型分类的公开问题。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子场论中的玻色场模型提供了一个统一的酉等价分类理论，填补了该领域（特别是红外重整化情形）的分类空白，其应用拓展到二次相互作用模型分类，是量子场论数学结构研究的重要进展。

Abstract: We classify a class of abstract Bose field models in quantum field theory up to unitary equivalence. The class includes abstract free Bose field models, abstract van Hove--Miyatake models, and infrared-renormalized van Hove--Miyatake models. Moreover, as an application of our classification, we classify quadratic interaction models. In particular, our results answer a question of A. Arai concerning the classification of infrared-renormalized van Hove--Miyatake models.

</details>


### [163] [Limits of Stochastic Semigroups and Block-Triangular Majorisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04057)
*Fabio Deelan Cunden,Jakub Czartowski,Giovanni Gramegna,Marilena Ligabò*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究由不变分布族定义的随机矩阵半群在参数趋近无穷时的极限行为，提出了一般的极限框架，并详细分析了不变分布为Gibbs向量（逆温度β）的情形。发现极限半群由带附加次随机约束的分块上三角随机矩阵构成，由此引出Block-Triangular支配关系，该关系介于经典支配和上三角支配之间，可由有限族单调量完全刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解随机矩阵半群与其不变分布之间的相互作用是否在极限操作下可交换。作者发现通常取极限后再保持极限分布与先保持分布再取极限这两种操作并不交换，因此需要建立系统的理论框架来研究这类极限半群的结构。

Method: 采用抽象框架定义参数化不变分布γ(β)所对应随机矩阵半群的极限概念；针对Gibbs分布的特定情形，利用热力学极限（逆温度β→∞）分析半群的结构，通过分块上三角分解与次随机约束刻画极限半群，并引入Block-Triangular支配关系，借助有限族单调量进行完整刻画，同时考察Rényi α-熵的极限行为。

Result: 极限半群的结构由分块上三角随机矩阵及附加的次随机性约束完全描述；对其极值元素进行了特征刻画和枚举；所得Block-Triangular支配介于经典支配与上三角支配之间，并可由有限族单调量完全刻画；Rényi α-熵在该极限下的行为也得到了分析。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了定义于参数化不变分布族上的随机矩阵半群极限的一般理论，揭示了半群取极限与不变分布取极限的不交换性，并引入了新的支配次序概念（Block-Triangular支配），为支配理论、随机矩阵半群与统计力学之间的联系提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We investigate limits of semigroups of stochastic matrices defined by their invariant distribution. Given probability vectors $γ(β)$ depending on a parameter $β$, we introduce a notion of convergence as $β\to\infty$ for the corresponding semigroups of $γ(β)$-preserving stochastic matrices and investigate the structure of the resulting limit. In general, the limiting semigroup differs from the semigroup preserving the limiting distribution, showing that these two operations do not commute. We develop a general framework for such limiting semigroups and study in detail the case in which the invariant distributions are Gibbs vectors at the inverse temperature $β$. We show that the limiting semigroup consists of block-upper-triangular stochastic matrices subject to additional substochasticity constraints. We characterise and enumerate their extremal elements and determine the preorder on probability vectors induced by the action of the semigroup. The resulting notion of Block-Triangular majorisation interpolates between ordinary majorisation and upper triangular (aka unordered) majorisation. We show that it is completely characterised by a finite family of monotones and analyse the corresponding behaviour of Rényi $α$-entropies as $β\to\infty$.

</details>


### [164] [A simple derivation of the zero-temperature BCS functional from the reduced BCS Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04060)
*Christian Hainzl,Riccardo Panza*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究全费米子Fock空间上的巨正则约化BCS哈密顿量，证明了有限体积基态能量与BCS泛函最小值之间的误差与体积无关，从而在热力学极限下BCS变分原理对基态能量密度是精确的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证BCS变分原理在热力学极限下对基态能量密度的精确性，为BCS理论提供严格的数学基础。

Method: 考虑具有吸引力配对相互作用的巨正则约化BCS哈密顿量，该相互作用支撑在费米面周围的能量壳层中。在固定化学势条件下，证明精确有限体积基态能量与有限体积BCS泛函最小值之间的差是体积无关的误差量。

Result: 证明了有限体积基态能量与BCS泛函最小值的差与体积无关（即不随体积增长），由此得出BCS变分原理在热力学极限下对基态能量密度是精确的。

Conclusion: BCS变分原理在热力学极限下严格成立，为超导BCS理论的变分方法提供了严格的数学证明。

Abstract: We consider a grand-canonical reduced BCS Hamiltonian on the full fermionic Fock space with an attractive pairing interaction supported in an energy shell around the Fermi surface. For fixed chemical potential, we prove that the exact finite-volume ground-state energy differs from the minimum of the corresponding finite-volume BCS functional by a volume-independent error. As a consequence, the BCS variational principle is exact for the ground-state energy density in the thermodynamic limit.

</details>

### [165] [Uncertainty quantification of fatigue initiation life for powder bed fusion metal additive manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03163)
*Yulin Guo,Veera Sundararaghavan,Boris Kramer*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一个从微观结构和缺陷不确定性直接传播到疲劳起始寿命分布的框架，利用EBSD和micro-CT表征数据驱动三个物理仿真，推导出疲劳起始寿命的闭式概率密度函数，并应用于316L不锈钢增材制造部件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于激光粉末床熔融增材制造的关键部件，量化的疲劳寿命预测对于资格认证至关重要。传统的确定性预测过于保守，需要一种能够传播微观结构和缺陷不确定性到疲劳寿命分布的方法。

Method: 从EBSD和micro-CT获得微观结构与缺陷表征数据，驱动三个物理仿真以获取弹性应变能释放率、裂纹路径表面能和疲劳指标参数。考虑这些量之间的高相关性（源于共享的微观结构），推导疲劳起始寿命的闭式概率密度函数。

Result: 应用于EOS M290激光粉末床熔融制造的316L不锈钢件，发现晶粒尺寸和孔隙分布均影响疲劳起始寿命分布。在固定总体孔隙体积分数下，较大晶粒导致疲劳起始寿命略微降低；多个小孔隙比相同总体积的少数大孔隙对疲劳寿命更有利。

Conclusion: 该框架通过提供疲劳起始寿命的解析分布而非保守的确定性预测，实现更明智的增材制造部件资格认证与部署决策。

Abstract: Predicting fatigue life with quantified uncertainties is essential for the qualification of critical components produced by laser-based powder bed fusion additive manufacturing. We present a framework that propagates microstructure and defect uncertainties directly to a fatigue initiation life distribution for a specific part. In particular, microstructure and defect characterizations are obtained from electron backscatter diffraction and micro-computed tomography scan data, which in turn inform three physics-based simulations yielding the fatigue-affecting quantities: the elastic energy release rate, the surface energy along the crack path, and the fatigue indicator parameter. Accounting for the uncertainties in these quantities and the high correlations among them due to the shared underlying microstructure, we derive a closed-form probability density function for the fatigue initiation life. This provides an analytical distribution instead of conservative deterministic predictions and enables more informed decision making for the qualification and deployment of additively manufactured components. Applying the framework to 316L stainless steel parts produced by an EOS M290 laser powder bed fusion machine, we find that both grain sizes and void distributions influence the fatigue initiation life distribution. Specifically, for a fixed total void volume fraction, larger grain sizes cause a marginal reduction in fatigue initiation life, and a population of many small voids is more favorable for fatigue life than fewer, larger voids of equivalent total volume.

</details>


### [166] [Natural Disasters and the Nonprofit Sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04136)
*Mayleen Cortez-Rodriguez*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该研究利用1991至2021年的县级面板数据及因果推断方法，考察自然灾害损失对县层面非营利组织结果的影响，发现与先前研究（发现灾害损失与非营利组织收入或资产呈小但正相关）不同，不存在因果效应的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管非营利部门在地方及国家经济以及灾害响应与恢复中扮演重要角色，灾害对其影响的研究却不足。作者希望填补这一空白，并纠正先前研究中可能存在的偏差。

Method: 使用覆盖1991至2021年的县级别面板数据集，采用适用于面板数据的因果推断方法（如双重差分、合成控制等）来分析自然灾害损失对县不同非营利组织结果的影响。

Result: 与先前发现灾害损失与非营利部门收入或资产呈小而正相关的研究相反，本文未发现灾害损失对县非营利组织结果存在因果效应的证据。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，先前观察到的灾害损失与非营利组织表现之间的关联可能并非因果关系，提示需要更审慎地看待灾害对非营利部门影响的经验证据。

Abstract: When natural disasters strike, individuals, communities, and even entire countries can suffer. Researchers have studied the impacts of disasters on various factors of interest, from mental health, to poverty, to economic activity. However, the impact of disasters on the nonprofit sector is understudied despite the nonprofit sector's perhaps surprising role in local or national economies as well as its role in disaster response and recovery. Thus, we study the effect of natural disaster damage on different county-level nonprofit outcomes using a panel dataset spanning 1991 to 2021 and causal inference methods tailored to panel data. Contrary to prior work, which found small but positive associations between disaster damage and nonprofit revenue or assets, we find no evidence of a causal effect.

</details>
